160	def _invoke_claude(prompt, timeout=600):
161	    """Launch ONE fresh headless Opus run. Returns (text, usage_dict). Never raises. `timeout` is the single-shot
162	    cap in seconds (raised by the verifier so a verify can finish — R-VERIFIER owner ruling 23 Sep)."""
163	    if not os.path.isfile(CLAUDE):
164	        return "", {"error": "claude CLI not found"}
165	    try:
166	        r = subprocess.run([CLAUDE, "--model", MODEL, "--output-format", "stream-json", "--verbose", "-p"],
167	                           input=prompt.encode("utf-8"), capture_output=True, timeout=timeout, cwd=REPO,
168	                           creationflags=0x08000000)     # R-NO-WINDOWS: CREATE_NO_WINDOW for the Opus sub-run
169	        out = r.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace")
170	    except Exception as e:
171	        return "", {"error": type(e).__name__}
172	    text, usage = [], {}
173	    for line in out.splitlines():
174	        try:
175	            ev = json.loads(line)
176	        except Exception:
177	            continue
178	        if ev.get("type") == "assistant":
179	            for b in (ev.get("message", {}) or {}).get("content", []):
180	                if isinstance(b, dict) and b.get("type") == "text":
181	                    text.append(b["text"])
182	        if ev.get("type") == "result":
183	            u = ev.get("usage") or {}
184	            in_tot = (u.get("input_tokens") or 0) + (u.get("cache_read_input_tokens") or 0) + \
185	                     (u.get("cache_creation_input_tokens") or 0)   # the big prompt is mostly cache-read
186	            usage = {"input": in_tot, "output": u.get("output_tokens"), "cost_usd": ev.get("total_cost_usd")}
187	    return "\n".join(text), usage

190	def _verdict(text):
191	    m = re.search(r"SUPERVISOR-VERDICT:\s*(ON-COURSE|VIOLATION)\s*(?:\|\s*(.*))?", text or "", re.I)
192	    if not m:
193	        return None, "no verdict line in the sub-run's reply"
194	    if m.group(1).upper() == "ON-COURSE":
195	        return "ON-COURSE", None
196	    return "VIOLATION", (m.group(2) or "unspecified").strip()

# LEAD-CHECKLIST (R-LEAD-FILES, END-TO-END BUNDLE Part 5) — verbatim

The supervisor scores every archived ruling against these rules and writes pass/fail per rule to the Queue
page beside the ruling (R-VERIFIER v2 step 7).

1. Eyes first: open the mirror, state its written time against the owner's phone time. Can't open it → say EYES GAP, never rule blind.
2. One ruling in flight. Nothing new until the verifier's PASS for the last one is on the mirror.
3. Report = claim. Verifier's run = exam. Never accept "done" from a report.
4. "Live" only when observed in the running loop by a session that didn't build it — line-cited in the loop the login launches, plus a dry run through it. Otherwise "built".
5. Builder ≠ verifier, different model. No session audits its own work.
6. Money math at design time: fees, fills, spread, exits, caps. Costs inside every exam.
7. Foresee where it breaks: every ruling carries a "where this can fail" line, written before the build.
8. No typed numbers. Measured only. ETAs machine-measured or "unmeasured".
9. Demos and tests never reach Telegram or a live ledger.
10. Core untouchable: login, launch, loop, orders change only through the nightly dry run with the 08:15 auto-revert. Plug-ins unlimited, each proven before the next.
11. Every dispatch: one path; no new process or hook unless it replaces one; archived to docs/dispatches before work; ends with the self-flag line.
12. Every observability ruling states per watcher: sees what · how old · what freezes it · what still needs the owner.
13. Engines by the owner's names. Blocks labelled → BUILD or → AUDIT. One paste a day outside emergencies.
14. Failed strategy → probation. Luck correction counts every trial, not the winners.

170	def _run_codex(prompt):
171	    """Rung (a): run the verify via Codex CLI non-interactively (codex.cmd exec). Returns the reply text, or None
172	    on any failure so the caller falls back to rung (d)."""
173	    if not os.path.isfile(CODEX):
174	        return None
175	    try:
176	        # prompt via STDIN ('-'): a ~15k-char prompt as a .cmd arg would hit cmd.exe's 8191-char limit.
177	        r = subprocess.run([CODEX, "exec", "-"], input=prompt, capture_output=True, text=True,
178	                           timeout=VERIFY_CAP, cwd=REPO, creationflags=0x08000000)
179	        out = (r.stdout or "") + (("\n" + r.stderr) if r.stderr else "")
180	        return out if out.strip() else None
181	    except Exception:
182	        return None

185	def _run_rung(rung, inputs):
186	    """Production brain. rung (a) = Codex (codex.cmd exec); rung (d) = fresh independent Claude (supervisor
187	    mechanism); (a) falls back to (d) on any Codex failure. Both are handed the pre-run FULL suite result +
188	    its hash and told NOT to re-run the ~21-min suite (it cannot finish in a single shot); they run only the
189	    fast TARGETED checks (module demo + touched-module tests) and confirm the suite hash."""
190	    import sys as _sys
191	    py = _sys.executable                                  # the real interpreter; the 'py' launcher is NOT installed
192	    suite_line, suite_blob = _suite_evidence()
193	    prompt = ("You are the INDEPENDENT VERIFIER. Follow docs/VERIFIER-TEMPLATE.md and run ONLY the TARGETED checks "
194	              "in docs/VERIFY-MAP.md for the touched paths. Run commands with THIS exact Python interpreter (the "
195	              "'py' launcher is NOT installed on this machine): %s\n"
196	              "Targeted checks to run and quote the output of (the -B avoids .pyc writes under a read-only sandbox):\n"
197	              "  1) \"%s\" -B -m engine1_rebuild.conform\n"
198	              "  2) \"%s\" -B -m pytest tests/test_conform.py -q -p no:cacheprovider\n"
199	              "DO NOT re-run the full ~21-min suite: its result was produced ALONE under the RAM gate and is given "
200	              "below as authoritative evidence — confirm the sha256 matches reports/CONFORM-SUITE-RESULT.json on "
201	              "disk. Do NOT modify, stage, or commit any file; run read-only checks only. A claim without a "
202	              "running-evidence line is FAIL. Reply with a first line 'VERDICT: PASS' or 'VERDICT: FAIL' then the "
203	              "evidence.\n\nPRE-RUN SUITE: %s\n%s\n\nRULING:\n%s\n\nCLAIMED STEP: %s\n\nCOMMIT %s DIFF:\n%s\n" %
204	              (py, py, py, suite_line, suite_blob[:1500], inputs["ruling"][:6000], inputs["step"], inputs["commit"],
205	               inputs["diff"]))
206	    out = None
207	    if rung == "a":
208	        cx = _run_codex(prompt)
209	        # Trust a Codex PASS, or a Codex FAIL where the checks ACTUALLY RAN. Fall back to rung (d) only when
210	        # Codex produced nothing OR its sandbox could not execute the checks (owner ruling: rung (d) fallback).
211	        if cx is not None and (re.search(r"(?im)^\s*VERDICT:\s*PASS\b", cx) or not _codex_blocked(cx)):
212	            out = cx
213	    if out is None:
214	        from scripts import supervisor
215	        out, _usage = supervisor._invoke_claude(prompt, timeout=VERIFY_CAP)   # (text, usage) -> unpack
216	    out = out or ""
217	    verdict = "PASS" if re.search(r"(?im)^\s*VERDICT:\s*PASS\b", out) else "FAIL"
218	    evidence = ((suite_line + "\n\n") if suite_line else "") + out            # pin the suite hash into the report
219	    return {"verdict": verdict, "evidence": evidence[:6000]}

Rulings: **R-CORPUS** (whole or not at all) and **R-PITUNIV** (how a machine knows).

WHAT THIS IS, IN ONE LINE
-------------------------
The thing that decides whether this repository is allowed to answer *"was this company
F&O-eligible on that date?"* at all — and, when it is not, says so instead of guessing.

WHY IT IS NOT A ROW COUNT
-------------------------
Part 4 row 4's original acceptance was *"`fo_membership_pit.csv` has rows."* On 2026-08-03
a run put 19 circulars of 81 into the folder an engine treats as complete evidence, and the
table promptly answered True/False about everything, read **RELIANCE, TCS and INFY as never
F&O-eligible**, and took the `G13-bias-2` badge off (**R-CORPUS**). A partial universe
answers in exactly the same voice as a complete one. So:

  COMPLETENESS IS DECLARED   a manifest travels WITH the corpus and names the document set,
                             each entry's publication instant and its content hash. No
                             manifest, no admission — a folder holding a quarter of a corpus
                             looks on disk exactly like a folder holding all of it.
  ADMISSION IS ATOMIC        one document missing, unreadable or byte-differing and ZERO are
                             admitted. The check runs to the end first, so the refusal names
                             every defect rather than the first.
  THE ANSWER IS COVERED      True/False on a date the admitted corpus covers (a covered date
                             is a COMPLETE snapshot, so a symbol it does not name is a
                             non-member); `None` everywhere else, never carried forward
                             (**R-FWDCAP1**). The uncovered dates are STATED.
  THE BADGE READS COVERAGE   `G13-bias-2` comes off on coverage and at no other moment
                             (`CLAUDE.md` §19). `engine1_technical/gate1.py` reads this
                             module's published state; an absent or unreadable state file
                             keeps the badge ON.

tool_result: def coverage(root=None):
    """What the ADMITTED corpus covers, and — stated, never interpolated — what it does not.

    Uncovered dates are CALENDAR dates. This repo holds no official trading calendar
    (`MEASUREMENT_CENSUS.md` Part 4 row 2), so a weekend inside the window is reported as
    uncovered rather than quietly excused — over-stating what is unknown, not under-stating.
    """
    state = check_corpus(root)
    covered = sorted(set(state["covers"])) if state["admitted_whole"] else []
    start, end = _day(state["window"].get("from")), _day(state["window"].get("to"))
    uncovered = []
    if start and end and start <= end:
        held = set(covered)
        day = start
        while day <= end:
            if day.isoformat() not in held:
                uncovered.append(day.isoformat())
            day += timedelta(days=1)
    return {"window": state["window"], "covered_dates": covered,
            "uncovered_dates": len(uncovered), "uncovered_ranges": _ranges(uncovered),
            "admitted_whole": state["admitted_whole"]}


def _ranges(days):
    """Contiguous ISO dates folded into [start, end] pairs — complete information rather
    than a truncated list, so nothing is dropped and no cut-off number is invented."""
    out = []
    for text in days:
        day = _day(text)
        if out and _day(out[-1][1]) + timedelta(days=1) == day:
            out[-1][1] = text
        else:
            out.append([text, text])
    return out

def _live_desk():
    """B4 + R-HALT-FIX §5: Live desk — BOOKED open positions and closed trades (engine on every row), with the
    held signals (fired but not booked) in a quiet section below. A trade appears only when booked."""
    opos, held = _booked_open_positions()
    orows = ""
    for p in opos:
        cls = "gain" if (p.get("mark_R") or 0) >= 0 else "loss"
        orows += ("<tr data-k='%s'><td>%s</td><td>%s</td><td>%s</td><td class='n'>%s</td><td class='n'>%s</td>"
                  "<td class='n %s'>%s</td><td class='n %s'>%s</td></tr>" % (
                      esc((str(p.get("stock", "")) + " " + str(p.get("engine", ""))).upper()),
                      esc(p.get("engine")), esc(p.get("stock")), esc(p.get("direction")),
                      esc(p.get("entry")), esc(p.get("stop")),
                      cls, esc(p.get("mark_R")), cls, R(p.get("uPnL"))))
    orows = orows or "<tr><td class='sub' colspan='8'>no booked positions — a trade appears only when booked</td></tr>"
    hrows = "".join("<tr><td>%s</td><td>%s</td><td>%s</td><td class='sub'>held — not booked</td></tr>" % (
        esc(p.get("engine")), esc(p.get("stock")), esc(p.get("direction"))) for p in held) \
        or "<tr><td class='sub' colspan='4'>no held signals</td></tr>"
    crows = ""
    for r in _closed_ledger_rows():
        rr = r.get("result_R")
        cls = "gain" if _num(rr) >= 0 else "loss"
        crows += ("<tr><td>E1</td><td>%s</td><td>%s</td><td>%s</td><td>%s</td><td class='n %s'>%s</td>"
                  "<td class='sub'>%s</td></tr>" % (
                      esc(r.get("stock")), esc(r.get("direction")), esc(r.get("entry_date")),
                      esc(r.get("exit_date")), cls, esc(rr), esc(r.get("exit_reason"))))
    crows = crows or "<tr><td class='sub' colspan='7'>no closed paper trades yet</td></tr>"
    body = (_head("Live desk — E1 desk") + _nav("live-desk.html") + "<main><h1>Live desk</h1>"
            "<p class='sub'>Every open and closed paper position, with the engine on each row. Paper only.</p>"
            "<div class='panel'><input id='fq' placeholder='filter open positions…' oninput=\"filt('fq','ot')\" "
            "style='width:100%%;padding:8px;background:var(--card);color:var(--ink);border:1px solid var(--line);"
            "border-radius:8px'></div>"
            "<h2>Open positions (%d)</h2><table id='ot'><thead><tr><th>Engine</th><th>Stock</th><th>Dir</th>"
            "<th>Entry</th><th>Stop</th><th>Now (R)</th><th>Unrealised</th></tr></thead><tbody>%s</tbody></table>"
            "<h2>Closed trades</h2><table><thead><tr><th>Engine</th><th>Stock</th><th>Dir</th><th>In</th>"
            "<th>Out</th><th>Result (R)</th><th>Why sold</th></tr></thead><tbody>%s</tbody></table>"
            "<h2 style='color:var(--ink3)'>Held signals (%d) — not trades</h2>"
            "<p class='sub'>Fired but not booked (the moment light was not green). A trade appears only when "
            "booked.</p><table><thead><tr><th>Engine</th><th>Stock</th><th>Dir</th><th></th></tr></thead>"
